Reference

Game Mode Studio nodes

Every node (643): what it does, its inputs and outputs, settings, limits and related nodes.

Made from the app's own catalog (nodes.json, nodehelp.json, limits.ts), so it always matches the app.

Events

Round Started #

Runs when a round begins: freeze time is over and everyone can move.

The usual place to set a round up: give weapons, reset scores, show a message. It runs once per round for the whole server, not per player (use For Each Player for that).

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Related: New Round, Freeze Time Ended, For Each Player

Round Ended #

Runs when a round ends. Winner is no team for a draw.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Winner Team
The team that won; no team for a draw.
Round number Number
How many rounds have been played on this map, counting this one.

Related: Round Started, Player Killed, Player Spawned

Player Killed #

Runs when a player dies. Attacker is nobody when the world killed them (a fall, the bomb).

Runs once for every death. Attacker is nobody when the world did it (a fall, the bomb, a trigger_hurt); check with Is Valid before giving the attacker anything.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Victim Player
Who died.
Attacker Player
Who killed them; nobody for the world, and the victim themselves for a suicide.
Headshot Yes/No
Yes when the killing shot hit the head.
Weapon Text
The weapon's short name, as the kill feed shows it: ak47, knife, grenade, world.
Distance Number
How far apart they were, in units (a player is 72 units tall).
Team kill Yes/No
Yes when the attacker shot a teammate.
Suicide Yes/No
Yes when the victim killed themselves (a grenade, the Kill node, the kill command).
Killed by the world Yes/No
Yes when no player did it.

Related: Player Hurt, Add Score, Respawn

Player Spawned #

Runs when a player comes alive (each round, or a respawn).

Runs each time a player comes alive: every round, and after Respawn. The place for loadouts and per-player setup.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Player Player
The player.

Related: Give Weapon, Strip Weapons, Set Health

Player Joined #

Runs when a player (or bot) has joined the server.

Runs once when someone (or a bot) has joined, before they pick a team. Per-player variables start fresh here.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Player Player
The player.

Related: Player Spawned, Player Left, Player Killed

Player Left #

Runs when a player leaves the server.

Runs as a player leaves. Their slot can be taken by someone else right after, so tidy up their variables here.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Player Player
The player.

Related: Player Joined, Bomb Planted, Player Spawned

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Planter Player
Planter: a player.

Related: Player Left, Bomb Defused, Player Joined

Runs when the bomb is defused.

Outputs

Related: Bomb Planted, Bomb Exploded, Player Left

Bomb Exploded #

Runs when the bomb goes off.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Related: Bomb Defused, Player Used, Bomb Planted

Player Used #

Runs when a player presses a button (func_button) with this name. Leave the name empty for any button.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Player Player
The player.
Button Entity
Button: an entity (a thing in the map).

Settings

Button name
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -).

Related: Bomb Exploded, Player Touched, Bomb Defused

Player Touched #

Runs when a player walks into a trigger (trigger_multiple) with this name, at most once a second each. Empty name: any.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Player Player
The player.
Trigger Entity
Trigger: an entity (a thing in the map).

Settings

Trigger name
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -).

Cost: Runs at most once a second per player, so a trigger can't flood the mode.

Related: Player Used, Bomb Exploded, Bomb Defused

Player Hurt #

Runs when a player takes damage, with the health it took (blocked damage doesn't count). Hit Position: where a bullet or the knife struck (else the middle of the victim); Direction: which way the hit was going (from the attacker). Use Get Transform on Victim/Attacker for where they were.

Runs after the damage is done, with what was really taken: nothing when it was blocked, less when armor took some. To change the damage before it lands, use Player About To Be Hurt.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Victim Player
Victim: a player.
Attacker Player
Attacker: a player.
Damage Number
Health taken by this hit.
Headshot Yes/No
Yes when a bullet or the knife hit the head.
Health left Number
Health left after it (0 or less: the hit killed them).
Hit where Text
Where a bullet hit: head, chest, stomach, arm, leg; body for anything else (grenades, falls).
Weapon Text
The weapon that did it: the attacker's gun or knife, hegrenade for a grenade, empty when no player did it.
Hit Position Vector
Hit Position: a position or direction (X, Y, Z).
Direction Vector
Direction: a position or direction (X, Y, Z).

Cost: Runs on every hit, often many times a second in a firefight. Keep what follows it small.

Related: Player About To Be Hurt, Player Killed, Team Changed

Team Changed #

Runs once when a player joins a team or changes team (Spectator is No team).

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Player Player
The player.
Team Team
The team.

Related: Player Hurt, Map Started, New Round

Outputs

Related: Team Changed, New Round, Player Hurt

New Round #

Runs when a new round is set up (freeze time starts), before Round Started.

Runs as the new round is being set up, before freeze time. Players are respawning, so per-player work is better done in Player Spawned.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Related: Map Started, Chat Command, Team Changed

Chat Command #

Runs when a player says /command or !command in chat (shop for /shop and !shop); Arguments is the rest of the line ("/give 5": 5).

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Player Player
The player.
Arguments Text
Whatever was typed after the command: for /give 5, the text 5.

Settings

Command (without /)
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -). Default: shop.

Related: New Round, Map Started, Team Changed

Team only Yes/No

Cost: Runs on every chat line. Fine for a few checks.

Related: Freeze Time Ended, Map Ending, Weapon Fired

Cost: Runs on every shot (at most ten times a second per player). Keep what follows it small, or a busy server slows down.

Runs before damage lands, so Damage nodes after it can scale or block it.

Cost: Runs on every hit. Keep what follows it small.

Custom Event #

Runs when something calls this Custom Event (Call Custom Event, Set Timer by Event, or the map: an ls_event entity, or an info_target named lsev_<its map name>, when triggered). Its inputs are its own (My mode, Custom events).

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Custom event
Picked from this mode's own pieces.

Related: Round Time Up, Downed Player Out, Player Revived

Entity Touched #

Runs when an entity touches another (props, projectiles, NPCs, creatures, players; not the world's walls and floors). Name: only an entity with this name (empty: any). The first touch per N seconds for each entity (players standing on a prop would touch it every frame).

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Entity Entity
The entity.
Other Entity
Other: an entity (a thing in the map).

Settings

Entity name
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -).
Only the first touch per (seconds)
A number from 0.1 to 60. Default: 1. From 0.1 up to 60.

Related: End Overlap, Bullet Hit, Begin Overlap

Bullet Hit #

Runs where a shot lands: the shooter, the hit position, the surface's normal, what it hit (an entity, or World), the weapon and, on a player, where (head, chest...). At most once per shooter every N seconds (automatic weapons and shotguns would flood it).

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Shooter Player
Shooter: a player.
Hit Position Vector
Hit Position: a position or direction (X, Y, Z).
Normal Vector
Normal: a position or direction (X, Y, Z).
Hit Entity Entity
Hit Entity: an entity (a thing in the map).
Hit World Yes/No
Hit World: yes or no.
Weapon Text
Weapon: text.
Hit where Text
Hit where: text.

Settings

At most once per shooter every (seconds)
A number from 0.05 to 10. Default: 0.1. From 0.05 up to 10.

Related: Entity Touched, End Overlap, Begin Overlap

Player Landed #

Runs when a player lands after a fall: how fast they were falling (units a second; over 580 hurts) and the fall damage the game gives for it (0 if Fall Damage Off). Small hops don't count.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Player Player
The player.
Fall speed Number
Fall speed: a number.
Damage Number
Damage: a number.

Settings

Only falls faster than
A number from 0 to 2000. Default: 250. From 0 up to 2000.

Related: Player Pressed Button, Bullet Hit, Player Command

Player Pressed Button #

Runs when a player presses (or lets go of) a button the game knows: fire, right click, use, jump, duck, reload, walk or a move key. It arrives with the player's ping, like everything they do. Only: wire a player (from a variable) to listen to one player only. Include bots: bots press buttons too.

Works the same on your own game, a listen server and a dedicated Linux server: the server sees a button when the player's game sends it, so it arrives with their ping (a 100 ms player's press shows up about 100 ms after they pressed). Only buttons the game itself has: fire, right click, use, jump, duck, reload, walk and the move keys. For any other key, use Player Command (players bind the key to it). Include bots: bots press buttons too (on by default); untick it for something only people should set off.

Inputs

Only this player Player
A player to listen to (from a variable, set when they're picked): nobody else's presses run it. Not wired: everyone's.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Player Player
Who pressed it.
Button Text
Which button, by name: attack, attack2, use, jump, duck, reload, walk, forward, back, left, right.

Settings

Button
Pick one: Attack (fire), Attack 2 (right click), Use (E), Jump, Duck, Reload, Walk (Shift), Forward, Back, Move left, Move right, Any of these. Default: use. Choices: Attack (fire), Attack 2 (right click), Use (E), Jump, Duck, Reload, Walk (Shift), Forward, Back, Move left, Move right, Any of these.
When
Pick one: Pressed, Released. Default: pressed. Choices: Pressed, Released.
Include bots
On or off. Default: true.

Cost: A check of each player's buttons every server frame, only when a mode has this node; the flow runs only on a press.

Related: Is Holding Button, Player Command, Start Cooldown

Player Command #

Runs when a player uses a console command the mode names (ls_ and letters, like ls_dash): they can bind any key to it (bind f "ls_dash"). Arguments is the rest of the line, as text only: it's never run. Each player can use it at most a few times a second (Player Command uses a second, in the mode's Limits settings).

A key the game has no button for (F, G, a mouse button) can still start a flow: name a command (ls_ and letters, like ls_dash), and players bind a key to it in their console (bind f "ls_dash"); Tell Player To Bind shows them the line. Arguments is whatever came after the command, as text only: the mode can read it, but it's never run as a command. Player Command uses a second (each player) keeps a held or scripted key from flooding the server: a setting in the mode's Limits group, 5 to start (the checks warn above 20).

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Player Player
Who used the command.
Arguments Text
The rest of the line, as text (empty when there was none).

Settings

Command (ls_...)
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -). Default: ls_dash.

Limits: Player Command uses a second (5 by default, hosts can raise it to 1000; slow above 20).

Careful: Safe by design: the command name can only be ls_ and letters (never one of the game's own), and the arguments are never run.

Related: Tell Player To Bind, Player Pressed Button, Chat Command

Picked Up #

Runs when a player picks a weapon or an item up from the ground (not when they buy one or the mode gives it). Item is its short name: ak47, kevlar, hegrenade...

Weapons and grenades from the ground (dropped ones, or a map's armoury), and armor from a map's armoury. Not what's bought, given by the mode, or handed out at spawn.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Player Player
Who picked it up.
Item Text
Its short name: ak47, deagle, hegrenade, kevlar.

Related: Dropped, Give Weapon, Player Command

Outputs

Weapon Text
Its short name: ak47, deagle, c4.

Settings

Also on death
On or off. Default: false.

Related: Picked Up, Drop Weapon, Player Command

Grenade Thrown #

Runs when a player throws a grenade: who, the grenade (an entity: Set Grenade Fuse, Push, Remove Entity work on it), which kind and where. Type: only that kind.

The position.

Settings

Type
Pick one: Any grenade, HE grenade, Flashbang, Smoke grenade. Default: any. Choices: Any grenade, HE grenade, Flashbang, Smoke grenade.

Related: Grenade Exploded, On Map Entity Fired, Weapon Switched

On Map Entity Fired #

Runs when a named entity of the map is triggered (by a button, a trigger, a timer or another entity) or used: its name, the entity, who set it off (a player, or nobody) and the entity that fired it. Name: only that entity (empty: any named one). Map logic drives the mode without an ls_event.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Entity name Text
Entity name: text.
Entity Entity
The entity.
Activator Player
Activator: a player.
Caller Entity
Caller: an entity (a thing in the map).

Settings

Entity name
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -).

Limits: Map entity kinds heard (96 by default, hosts can raise it to 256; slow above 160).

Related: Weapon Switched, Grenade Exploded, Grenade Thrown

Weapon Switched #

Runs when a player takes out a weapon (a switch, a pick-up that goes to hand, a spawn): the player, the new weapon and the one before (short names: ak47, knife; empty when there was none), and the weapon entity.

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

New weapon Text
New weapon: text.
Old weapon Text
Old weapon: text.

Weapon entity: an entity (a thing in the map).

Related: On Map Entity Fired, Entered Map Zone, Left Map Zone

On Tick #

Runs again and again, every Interval seconds (0.1: ten times a second; 0: as often as the mode's Limits setting lets it, 100 a second at first), all through the map. Delta is the seconds since the last time.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Delta Number
Delta: a number.

Settings

Interval (seconds)
A number from 0 to 60. Default: 0.1. From 0 up to 60.

Limits: On Tick runs a second at most (100 by default, hosts can raise it to 1000; slow above 100).

Related: Left Map Zone, Entered Map Zone, Weapon Switched

Conditions

Is Team #

Checks which team a player is on. Nobody (an empty attacker) is on no team, so that goes to False.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Player Player
The player. Needs a wire.
Team Team
The team. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

True Flow
Runs when the answer is yes.
False Flow
Runs when the answer is no.

Settings

Team
Pick one: Terrorists, Counter-Terrorists. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: t. Choices: Terrorists, Counter-Terrorists.

Related: Branch, Compare, And / Or

Branch #

Goes to True or False.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Condition Yes/No
Condition: yes or no. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

True Flow
Runs when the answer is yes.
False Flow
Runs when the answer is no.

Settings

Condition
On or off. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: true.

Related: Is Team, Compare, And / Or

Compare #

Compares two numbers.

Inputs

A Number
A: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.
B Number
B: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Result Yes/No
Result: yes or no.

Settings

A
A number from -100000 to 100000. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 0. From -100000 up to 100000.
Is
Pick one: > more than, ≥ at least, < less than, ≤ at most, = equal to, ≠ not equal to. Default: gt. Choices: > more than, ≥ at least, < less than, ≤ at most, = equal to, ≠ not equal to.
B
A number from -100000 to 100000. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 0. From -100000 up to 100000.

Related: Branch, And / Or, Is Team

And / Or #

Both (And), or either one (Or).

Inputs

A Yes/No
A: yes or no. Leave it empty to use the setting.
B Yes/No
B: yes or no. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Result Yes/No
Result: yes or no.

Settings

A
On or off. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: true.
Needs
Pick one: both (And), either (Or). Default: and. Choices: both (And), either (Or).
B
On or off. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: true.

Related: Compare, Not, Branch

Not #

True becomes False and False becomes True.

Inputs

In Yes/No
In: yes or no. Needs a wire.

Outputs

Result Yes/No
Result: yes or no.

Related: And / Or, Compare, Branch

Outputs

Settings

Same as
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Up to 60.

Related: Not, And / Or, Compare

Outputs

Settings

Holds
What kind of values it holds. Default: number.
Is
Pick one: equal, not equal. Default: eq. Choices: equal, not equal.

Related: Select, Is Valid, Is Valid?

Related: Select, Equal, Is Valid?

Nearly Equal #

Are A and B within Tolerance of each other (for numbers that came out of math, which are seldom exactly equal)?

Inputs

A Number
A: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.
B Number
B: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Tolerance Number
Tolerance: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Nearly equal Yes/No
Nearly equal: yes or no.

Settings

A
A number from -100000 to 100000. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 0. From -100000 up to 100000.
B
A number from -100000 to 100000. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 0. From -100000 up to 100000.
Tolerance

While Loop #

Runs Loop Body while the condition is true (checked each time; at most Limit times), then Completed.

Repeats Body while the condition is true. Limit stops it after that many times, so a condition that never turns false can't freeze the server.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Condition Yes/No
Condition: yes or no. Needs a wire.

Outputs

Loop Body Flow
Runs once for every step.
Completed Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Limit
A number from 1 to 10000. Default: 1000. From 1 up to 10000.

Careful: A loop that never ends is stopped by Limit; the Problems panel says when that happened.

Related: For Loop with Break, For Loop, Switch on Number

Around point: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Around entity Entity
Around entity: an entity (a thing in the map).
Radius Number
Radius: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Players array<player>
Players: a list of players.

Settings

Radius
A number from 1 to 8192. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 300. From 1 up to 8192.
Alive only
On or off. Default: true.
Team
Pick one: Any team, Terrorists, Counter-Terrorists. Default: any. Choices: Any team, Terrorists, Counter-Terrorists.

Related: Nearest Player, Entities In Radius, Nearest Entity

Entities In Radius #

Entities within Radius of a point or an entity, of a class or any, as an array (up to the mode's Limits setting, 64 at first). Players aren't listed (use Players In Radius).

Inputs

Around point: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.

Around entity: an entity (a thing in the map).
Radius Number

Outputs

Settings

Radius
A number from 1 to 32768. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 300. From 1 up to 32768.
Class (empty: any)
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -).

Limits: Entities one search keeps (64 by default, hosts can raise it to 2048; slow above 256).

Related: Nearest Player, Nearest Entity, Players In Radius

B: an entity (a thing in the map).
Or point B Vector
Or point B: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.

Hit position Vector
Hit position: a position or direction (X, Y, Z).

Settings

Creatures and players block
On or off. Default: false.

Related: Nearest Entity, Entities In Radius, Nearest Player

Find Free Spot Near #

A spot near a point where a player (or the hull you pick) fits: the point itself if it's free, else the nearest free one in rings round it, up to Max distance (at most the mode's Limits setting of tries, 64 at first). Found is no when there's none (Position is then the point).

Inputs

Near Vector
Near: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Needs a wire.

Outputs

Found Yes/No
Found: yes or no.
Position Vector
The position.

Settings

Size
Pick one: Player standing, Player ducking, Large (big creatures), A point. Default: stand. Choices: Player standing, Player ducking, Large (big creatures), A point.
Max distance
A number from 0 to 4096. Default: 256. From 0 up to 4096.

Limits: Tries finding a free spot (64 by default, hosts can raise it to 1024; slow above 256).

Related: Line of Sight, Nearest Entity, Entities In Radius

Timers

Delay #

Waits, then carries on. Paused with the world while debugging.

Waits, then goes on. Other things keep running while it waits. Running it again before it's done starts another wait.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Seconds Number
Seconds: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Timer Timer
The timer.

Settings

Seconds
A number from 0.1 to 3600. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 3. From 0.1 up to 3600.

Related: Repeat, Countdown, Stop Timers

Repeat #

Runs Each every few seconds, a number of times (0: until the round ends). Then runs at once.

Runs Tick every so many seconds, Times times (0: until the round ends or you stop it). Then runs right away.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Every (s) Number
Every (s): a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Each Flow
Runs every time the timer fires.
Timer Timer
The timer.

Settings

Every (seconds)
A number from 0.1 to 3600. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 5. From 0.1 up to 3600.
Times (0 = until the round ends)
A number from 0 to 10000. Default: 0. From 0 up to 10000.

Cost: A short interval with a lot after Tick is the most common way a mode gets heavy. Half a second or more is usually plenty.

Related: Delay, Countdown, Stop Timers

Outputs

Settings

Text
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. Default: Starting in. Up to 60.

Pick one: Top middle, Left, Right, Middle. Default: top. Choices: Top middle, Left, Right, Middle.

Related: Repeat, Stop Timers, Delay

Timeline #

Plays a Timeline (My mode, Timelines): values that change over time (a number, a position, a color) and events at set moments. Update runs every tick while it plays; Finished at the end. In a Function with a Player input, each player has their own. Double-click to edit its tracks.

Each key says how the track goes to the next: Linear, Constant (a step), Ease In, Ease Out, Ease In-Out, or Custom, where you drag the key's tangent handles to shape the curve (Alt drags one side; Auto tangents puts them back). The plugin works the curves out with a few multiplications a tick, no tables.

Inputs

Play Flow
Runs this node.
Play from Start Flow
Runs Play from Start.
Stop Flow
Runs Stop.
Reverse Flow
Runs Reverse.
Reverse from End Flow
Runs Reverse from End.
Set New Time Flow
Runs Set New Time.
New Time Number
New Time: a number.

Outputs

Update Flow
Runs on Update.
Finished Flow
Runs on Finished.
Forward Yes/No
Forward: yes or no.
Time Number
Time: a number.

Settings

Timeline
Picked from this mode's own pieces.

Cost: A Timeline in a Function with a Player input runs once per player: up to 32 copies, each ticking 20 times a second at the default Tick.

Related: Clear Timer, Is Timer Active, Set Timer by Event

Clear Timer #

Give Weapon #

Gives a living player a weapon and spare bullets. Does nothing for a dead player, or one who already has that weapon (the debugger marks that). A weapon's name can be wired in (from a data table, say): ak47, deagle, knife...

Gives the weapon with reserve ammo. A player who already has one in that slot gets it as well; Strip first for a clean loadout.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Player Player
The player. Needs a wire.
Weapon Text
Weapon: text. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Spare bullets Number
Spare bullets: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Weapon
Pick one: AK-47, M4A1, AWP, Scout, FAMAS, Galil, AUG, SG 552, MP5, P90, UMP 45, MAC-10…. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: ak47. Choices: AK-47, M4A1, AWP, Scout, FAMAS, Galil, AUG, SG 552, MP5, P90, UMP 45, MAC-10, TMP, M3 shotgun, XM1014 shotgun, M249, Desert Eagle, USP, Glock, P228, Five-SeveN, Dual Elites, HE grenade, Flashbang, Smoke grenade.
Spare bullets
A number from 0 to 250. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 90. From 0 up to 250.

Related: Strip Weapons, Set Health, Set Armor

Player By Steam ID #

The connected player with this Steam ID (STEAM_0:1:2345, as Steam ID gives it), or none; Found says which. Case and the STEAM_0 / STEAM_1 digit don't matter (the same player either way). Bots and LAN players have no Steam ID.

Push #

Pushes a player or an entity: along where they aim, where they face (flat), the Direction wired in, away from a Point, or straight up, by Strength (units a second). Add keeps their speed and adds to it; Replace sets it. Lift adds a little upward push so people on the ground aren't held by friction. Jump pads, dashes, knockback.

Runs this node.
Player or entity Entity

Strength
A number from 0 to 3000. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 500. From 0 up to 3000.
Lift
A number from 0 to 1000. Default: 0. From 0 up to 1000.

Pick one: Add to their speed, Replace their speed. Default: add. Choices: Add to their speed, Replace their speed.

Related: Player Movement State, Jump Rules, Voice Override

Jump Rules #

Jumping rules for a player (or everyone): block jumping or ducking, extra jumps in the air (double jump and more), and automatic bunny hopping (hold jump to hop with no slowdown). Their own game predicts the stock moves, so a blocked jump can look like a small hop on their screen.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Player (empty: everyone) Player
Player (empty: everyone): a player. Optional.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Block jumping
On or off. Default: false.
Block ducking
On or off. Default: false.
Extra air jumps
A number from 0 to 10. Default: 1. From 0 up to 10.
Air jump strength
A number from 100 to 1000. Default: 268. From 100 up to 1000.
Auto bunny hop
On or off. Default: false.
Back to normal on death
On or off. Default: false.
Back to normal at a new round
On or off. Default: false.

Related: Push, Player Movement State, Voice Override

Related: Player Pressed Button, Tell Player To Bind

Tell Player To Bind #

Shows how to bind a key to a Player Command: the line to type in the console (bind f "ls_dash"). Nothing is bound for them: the game never lets a server change a player's keys.

The game never lets a server change a player's keys, so this only tells them what to type: it shows the line in chat. Nothing is bound for them.

A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -). Default: ls_dash.

Related: Player Command, Is Holding Button

Cooldowns

Start Cooldown #

Starts a cooldown by name (like dash) for Seconds: for each player, each team or everyone. Is Ready says no until it's over.

One cooldown per ability name: for each player, each team, or one for everyone. Start it when the ability is used, check Is Ready before letting it run again. A player's cooldowns start clean when they leave; a new map starts them all clean.

Runs this node.

Seconds
A number from 0 to 86400. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 5. From 0 up to 86400.

Related: Is Ready, Cooldown Time Left, Reset Cooldown, Player Pressed Button

Weapons

Infinite Ammo #

Keeps a player's ammo full: the clip (no reloading), the spare ammo, or both. Reset on death: back to normal when they die.

The clip: every shot puts the bullet back, so they never reload. Spare ammo: topped up to the most that gun can carry whenever it changes. Works with every gun; grenades aren't ammo.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Player Player
The player. Needs a wire.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

On
On or off. Default: true.
Keep full
Pick one: The clip, Spare ammo, Both. Default: both. Choices: The clip, Spare ammo, Both.
Reset on death
On or off. Default: true.

Related: Fire Rate, Recoil

Fire Rate #

Makes a player's guns fire faster (2 = twice as fast) or slower (0.5 = half). 1 is normal. Reset on death: back to 1 when they die.

Changes the time between shots after each shot, so it follows every gun's own speed (2 makes an AK-47 fire twice as fast). Very high values can look odd: animations are the gun's own.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Player Player
The player. Needs a wire.
Multiplier Number
Multiplier: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Multiplier
A number from 0.1 to 10. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 2. From 0.1 up to 10.
Reset on death
On or off. Default: true.

Cost: A hook on each gun's fire, only when a mode has a weapon tuning node.

Related: Recoil, Infinite Ammo

Recoil #

Scales how much a player's view kicks when they fire: 0 none, 0.5 half, 1 normal, 2 double. Reset on death: back to 1 when they die.

Scales the view kick each shot gives. Spread (where bullets go) is the game's own and doesn't change.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Player Player
The player. Needs a wire.
Multiplier Number
Multiplier: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Multiplier
A number from 0 to 5. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 0.5. From 0 up to 5.
Reset on death
On or off. Default: true.

Related: Fire Rate, Infinite Ammo

Deal Damage #

Hurts a player or any entity that can be hurt (a breakable, an NPC, a creature) by Amount, as if Attacker did it: kills, the kill feed, scores and Player Hurt / Player Killed count as usual. No Attacker: the world did it. Kind is what it counts as (a burn, a fall...); Hit zone is where (a head hit shows as a headshot in the kill feed; the amount isn't multiplied). Armor takes its share unless Ignore armor.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Target Entity
Target: an entity (a thing in the map). Needs a wire.
Attacker Player
Attacker: a player. Optional.
Amount Number
Amount: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Amount
A number from 0 to 10000. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 25. From 0 up to 10000.
Kind
Pick one: Generic, Bullet, Slash (knife), Burn, Blast, Fall, Shock, Crush, Poison, Freeze. Default: generic. Choices: Generic, Bullet, Slash (knife), Burn, Blast, Fall, Shock, Crush, Poison, Freeze.
Hit zone
Pick one: Anywhere (generic), Head, Chest, Stomach, Left arm, Right arm, Left leg, Right leg. Default: generic. Choices: Anywhere (generic), Head, Chest, Stomach, Left arm, Right arm, Left leg, Right leg.
Ignore armor
On or off. Default: false.

Related: Set Damage, Scale Damage, Block Damage

Top Players #

The best players by a saved value (Saved Value's name), highest first or lowest first, offline players too (the save file keeps their names): Each runs once per place with Rank, Name and Value, then Then. How many places: Top Players places, in the mode's Limits settings (10 to start).

Reads every player's value of one Saved Value (its name) from the save file, so players who aren't on the server count too, named as they were last seen. Each runs once per place, best first. How many places is Top Players places, a setting in the mode's Limits group, 10 to start (the checks warn above 50).

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.

Outputs

Each Flow
Runs once for every item.
Rank Number
The place: 1 for the best.
Name Text
Their name as last seen (bots and LAN players: the name they were saved by).
Value Number
Their saved value.
Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Saved value
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -). Default: wins.
Order
Pick one: Highest first, Lowest first. Default: high. Choices: Highest first, Lowest first.

Limits: Top Players places (10 by default, hosts can raise it to 100; slow above 50).

Cost: Goes through the whole save file each time it runs: run it now and then (a round's start, a chat command), not every tick.

Related: Saved Value, Add To Saved, Save Value

Log Line #

Writes a line to the AMX Mod X log (addons/amxmodx/logs), at most Most a second from this node (the rest are counted in the next line).

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Text Text
The text. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Text
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: Something happened. Up to 120.
Most a second
A number from 1 to 100. Default: 5. From 1 up to 100.

Related: Show Info Window, Play Radio, Colored Chat

Game

Set Setting #

Changes one of the game's settings now.

A setting changed for the rest of the map. For settings the mode always wants, add them under This mode's Game settings instead (set every map).

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Value Number
Value: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Setting
Pick one: Free for all (1 on), Friendly fire (1 on), Gravity (800 normal), Buy time (minutes), Start money, Air control (10 normal), Round time (minutes, next round), Freeze time (seconds, next round), Rounds only end when the mode says (1 on), Team balance (1 on), Most players more than the other team (0 any), Bomb for the Terrorists (1 on)…. Default: mp_freeforall. Choices: Free for all (1 on), Friendly fire (1 on), Gravity (800 normal), Buy time (minutes), Start money, Air control (10 normal), Round time (minutes, next round), Freeze time (seconds, next round), Rounds only end when the mode says (1 on), Team balance (1 on), Most players more than the other team (0 any), Bomb for the Terrorists (1 on), Downed players can be finished by enemies (1 on; components), A downed player's health when they can be finished (components).
Value
A number from 0 to 100000. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 1. From 0 up to 100000.

Careful: A few settings are set again by the game's own game.cfg after the mode's, so they don't stick: the bomb for the Terrorists (mp_give_player_c4) is one. Take the bomb with Remove Weapon (c4) at Round Started instead.

Related: Use Entity, Current Map Name, Next Map

Remove Map Objectives #

Takes the map's objectives away (tick which): buy zones, bomb sites, hostages, hostage rescue zones, VIP safety zones, escape zones. The game notices at the next round (the bomb isn't given, no hostages to save); removed buy zones don't fall back to buying at spawn. Best at Map Started.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Buy zones
On or off. Default: false.
Bomb sites
On or off. Default: true.
Hostages
On or off. Default: true.
Rescue zones
On or off. Default: true.
VIP safety zones
On or off. Default: true.
Escape zones
On or off. Default: true.

Related: Map Type, Voice Rules, Round End Rules

Has VIP zone: yes or no.

Voice Rules #

Who hears whom on voice chat: everyone, only teammates, the dead only the dead and the living only the living, or only those within a distance (0: any distance, with the other rules). Spectators hear spectators. Voice Override changes it for one player; Mute Voice still mutes.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Who hears
Pick one: Everyone hears everyone, Teammates only, Dead with dead, alive with alive, Teammates, and dead with dead, Only within the distance. Default: team. Choices: Everyone hears everyone, Teammates only, Dead with dead, alive with alive, Teammates, and dead with dead, Only within the distance.
Within (units, 0: any)
A number from 0 to 8192. Default: 0. From 0 up to 8192.

Related: Map Type, Remove Map Objectives, Round End Rules

Round End Rules #

Stops rounds ending by themselves: when a team is wiped out, when the time runs out, or either (End Round is then the only way a round ends). Ending as usual undoes it; a new mode starts as usual.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Rounds don't end when
Pick one: (Ending as usual), A team is wiped out, Time runs out, Either (only End Round ends them). Default: elim. Choices: (Ending as usual), A team is wiped out, Time runs out, Either (only End Round ends them).

Related: Start Vote, Vote Count, Voice Rules

Start Vote #

Asks everyone (or a team, or the living) a question with 2 to 8 answers for some seconds; then runs the winning answer's output, Tie, or No votes. Bots vote at random if Bots vote is on. Only one vote at a time, and never over the server's map or mode vote (then No votes runs). Vote Count reads the result.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
1 Flow
Runs on 1.
2 Flow
Runs on 2.
3 Flow
Runs on 3.
4 Flow
Runs on 4.
5 Flow
Runs on 5.
6 Flow
Runs on 6.
7 Flow
Runs on 7.
8 Flow
Runs on 8.
Tie Flow
Runs on Tie.
No votes Flow
Runs when nothing was found.

Settings

Question
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. Default: Next round?. Up to 60.
Answers
A number from 2 to 8. Default: 2. From 2 up to 8.
Answer 1
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. Default: Knives. Up to 30.
Answer 2
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. Default: Pistols. Up to 30.
Answer 3
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. Default: Rifles. Up to 30.
Answer 4
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. Default: Snipers. Up to 30.
Answer 5
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. Default: Shotguns. Up to 30.
Answer 6
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. Default: Grenades. Up to 30.
Answer 7
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. Default: Random. Up to 30.
Answer 8
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. Default: Normal. Up to 30.
Seconds
A number from 5 to 60. Default: 15. From 5 up to 60.
Who votes
Pick one: Everyone, Terrorists, Counter-Terrorists, The living. Default: all. Choices: Everyone, Terrorists, Counter-Terrorists, The living.
Bots vote
On or off. Default: false.

Related: Vote Count, Round End Rules, Voice Rules

Bots: Phase #

Tells the bots what part of the game it is: Loot (nobody is an enemy: they gear up and keep apart), Fight, or back to normal. Personality influence: 0 they obey exactly; 50 as usual; 100 each bot's personality and skill decide (a careful one may turn a risky job down, a slow one starts later).

Runs this node.

Personality influence: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Pick one: Loot (no enemies), Fight, Normal. Default: loot. Choices: Loot (no enemies), Fight, Normal.
Personality influence (0-100)
A number from 0 to 100. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 50. From 0 up to 100.

Needs Loner-Strike's components plugin, which comes with the app.

Bots Avoid Area #

Marks a place bots keep away from: a Position with a radius, or At entity (it rides along: a carrier, a burning player, a trigger volume; it ends when that's gone). Strength 1-10: 5 and up they won't stand in it, below they only path round it. Seconds: 0 till Bots Stop Avoiding. Which bots: every bot, or one team's (the carrier's own team can be left out). The same tag again moves or changes it. The bot brain keeps 32 at once.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Position Vector
The position.
At entity Entity
At entity: an entity (a thing in the map). Optional.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Tag
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -). Default: danger.
Radius
A number from 16 to 4096. Default: 160. From 16 up to 4096.
Strength
A number from 1 to 10. Default: 6. From 1 up to 10.
Seconds (0: till stopped)
A number from 0 to 3600. Default: 0. From 0 up to 3600.
Which bots
Pick one: Every bot, Terrorist bots, Counter-Terrorist bots. Default: all. Choices: Every bot, Terrorist bots, Counter-Terrorist bots.

Limits: Areas bots avoid at once (16 by default, hosts can raise it to 32; slow above 24).

Needs Loner-Strike's components plugin, which comes with the app.

Related: Bots Stop Avoiding, Add Bots, Remove Bots

Related: Bots Avoid Area, Add Bots, Remove Bots

Add Bots #

Adds bots through the bot brain's own commands: how many, to which team, how good (new bots only). Never more than the free slots (one is kept open for a person). The host's bot count comes back at the next map.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
How many Number
How many: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

How many
A number from 1 to 31. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 2. From 1 up to 31.
Team
Pick one: Either (the game balances), Terrorists, Counter-Terrorists. Default: any. Choices: Either (the game balances), Terrorists, Counter-Terrorists.
Difficulty
Pick one: The host's setting, Easy, Normal, Hard, Expert. Default: host. Choices: The host's setting, Easy, Normal, Hard, Expert.

Related: Remove Bots, Bots Stop Avoiding, Bots Avoid Area

Remove Bots #

Takes bots off the server (of a team, or any), lowering the bot count so they don't come back this map. The host's bot count comes back at the next map.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
How many Number
How many: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

How many
A number from 1 to 31. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 1. From 1 up to 31.
Team
Pick one: Any team, Terrorists, Counter-Terrorists. Default: any. Choices: Any team, Terrorists, Counter-Terrorists.

Related: Add Bots, Bots Stop Avoiding, Bots Avoid Area

Create Component #

Makes a component at an entity (wired into At, or the one with this name), with keys like radius=160 capture_secs=8. Where: At entity (its spot, moved by Local offset along its own forward, right and up; it faces the way the entity does if Face the way At entity faces is on, turned by Rotation offset) or At position. Rotation (pitch, yaw, roll) sets the facing outright (no Velocity or Scale: a component is a zone).

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
At entity Entity
At entity: an entity (a thing in the map). Optional.
At position Vector
At position: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Local offset (forward, right, up) Vector
Local offset (forward, right, up): a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Rotation (pitch, yaw, roll) Vector
Rotation (pitch, yaw, roll): a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Rotation offset Vector
Rotation offset: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Component Entity
Component: an entity (a thing in the map).

Settings

Component
Pick one: Capture point, Safe zone, Shrinking zone, Damage zone, Loot container, Weapon spawner, Supply drop, Wave spawner, Extraction point, Checkpoint, Trap, Respawn point…. Default: ls_capture_point. Choices: Capture point, Safe zone, Shrinking zone, Damage zone, Loot container, Weapon spawner, Supply drop, Wave spawner, Extraction point, Checkpoint, Trap, Respawn point, Shop (a counter, items=Name|price|give|what;...), Perk machine, Upgrade bench, Mystery box, NPC (model=barney name=... behavior=stand), Interactable (prompt=... once=1).
At the entity named
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -). Default: spot1.
Keys
Settings for the component, as key=value pairs. Up to 400.
Face the way At entity faces
On or off. Default: true.

Needs Loner-Strike's components plugin, which comes with the app.

Careful: Each component is an entity; remove the ones you're done with.

Related: Find Component, Set Zone, Shrink Zone

Runs this node.

The player. Needs a wire.

Outputs

Needs Loner-Strike's components plugin, which comes with the app.

At once per player
A number from 1 to 8. Default: 1. From 1 up to 8.
Creature type
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -). Default: turret.
Bots buy them
On or off. Default: true.

Set Weather #

Rain, snow or clear weather for everyone or one player, kept through respawns (Counter-Strike 1.6 and Condition Zero draw it; how much depends on the player's settings).

Players with weather effects turned off in their options see nothing; it's only looks (no sounds, no slowdown).

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Player (empty: everyone) Player
Player (empty: everyone): a player. Optional.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Weather
Pick one: Clear, Rain, Snow. Default: rain. Choices: Clear, Rain, Snow.

Limits: Effect messages a frame (48 by default, hosts can raise it to 160; slow above 96).

Related: Set Fog, Set Frag Limit, Frag Limit

Set Fog #

Fog of a color and thickness for everyone or one player (0 thickness: none), kept through respawns. It depends on the player's renderer: OpenGL draws it, software mode doesn't.

OpenGL draws fog; the software renderer doesn't. Thickness 0.001 to 0.005 is a light haze; 0.02 and up hides things a short way off.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Player (empty: everyone) Player
Player (empty: everyone): a player. Optional.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Red
A number from 0 to 255. Default: 128. From 0 up to 255.
Green
A number from 0 to 255. Default: 128. From 0 up to 255.
Blue
A number from 0 to 255. Default: 128. From 0 up to 255.
Thickness
A number from 0 to 0.05. Default: 0.002. From 0 up to 0.05.

Limits: Effect messages a frame (48 by default, hosts can raise it to 160; slow above 96).

Careful: Thick fog hides players from people but not from bots.

Related: Set Weather, Set Frag Limit, Frag Limit

Find Entities #

Every entity of a class or with a name, as an array (at most 512). Looks through the whole map: keep it in a variable instead of in a loop.

Outputs

Entities array<entity>
Entities: a list of entity (thing in the map)s.

Settings

By
Pick one: Class, Name. Default: class. Choices: Class, Name.
Class or name
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -). Default: info_target.

Cost: Searches the whole map. Find once (at a round's start) and keep the list in a variable instead of finding again and again.

Related: Entity Name Of, Spawn Prop, Entity Class Of

Spawn Prop #

A model (models/...mdl, loaded with the map) at an entity's place; solid or not. Where: At entity (its spot, moved by Local offset along its own forward, right and up; it faces the way the entity does if Face the way At entity faces is on, turned by Rotation offset) or At position. Rotation (pitch, yaw, roll) sets the facing outright; Velocity starts it moving (a still thing then falls and slides); Scale sizes models and sprites.

Puts a model in the world at an entity's place. The model is loaded when the map loads, so it must be a file the server and players have.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
At entity Entity
At entity: an entity (a thing in the map). Optional.
At position Vector
At position: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Local offset (forward, right, up) Vector
Local offset (forward, right, up): a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Rotation (pitch, yaw, roll) Vector
Rotation (pitch, yaw, roll): a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Rotation offset Vector
Rotation offset: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Velocity Vector
Velocity: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Scale Number
Scale: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Prop Entity
Prop: an entity (a thing in the map).

Settings

Model
A file in the game folder, like models/w_c4.mdl or ambience/thunder_clap.wav. Default: models/w_c4.mdl.
Solid
On or off. Default: true.
Face the way At entity faces
On or off. Default: true.
Scale
A number from 0.05 to 10. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 1. From 0.05 up to 10.

Careful: Each prop is an entity; the game has a limit (about 900). Remove props you don't need any more.

Related: Find Entities, Remove Entity, Entity Name Of

Outputs

Then Flow

Get Bone Position #

Where a bone of a player's or any model entity's skeleton is now (like Bip01 R Hand or Bip01 Head), and which way it points. Model is only for the list of bones; the bone is looked up by name in the model the entity really has, and Found is No when it has no such bone. Positions follow the server's animation: close, not pixel-exact.

The bone is found by name in the entity's own model each time (a player is models/player.mdl on the server, whatever skin they wear), so one node works for every player. The server poses the model from its own animation, which is close to what players see but not exact.

Inputs

Entity Entity
The entity. Needs a wire.

Outputs

Position Vector
The position.
Angles Vector
Pitch, yaw and roll of the bone.
Found Yes/No
No when the entity's model has no bone with that name, or it isn't a studio model.

Settings

Model (for the list)
A file in the game folder, like models/w_c4.mdl or ambience/thunder_clap.wav. Default: models/player.mdl.
Bone
Picked from this mode's own pieces. Default: Bip01 R Hand.

Related: Get Attachment Position, Distance, Get Entity Property

Get Attachment Position #

Where an attachment point of an entity's model is now (a gun's muzzle, a hand; models number them 0 to 3), and its angles. Model is only for the list; Found is No when the entity's real model has no such attachment. Positions follow the server's animation: close, not pixel-exact.

Attachments are a model's fixed points (a weapon's muzzle and shell ejection, a hand): most have no names, so the list shows each one's number and the bone it sits on.

Inputs

Entity Entity
The entity. Needs a wire.

Outputs

Position Vector
The position.
Angles Vector
Angles: a position or direction (X, Y, Z).
Found Yes/No
No when the entity's model has fewer attachments, or it isn't a studio model.

Settings

Model (for the list)
A file in the game folder, like models/w_c4.mdl or ambience/thunder_clap.wav. Default: models/player.mdl.
Attachment
Picked from this mode's own pieces. Default: 0.

Related: Get Bone Position, Get Entity Property, Set Entity Property

Changes a property of any entity: render mode, effect and color (so props and NPCs can glow or fade), amount, skin, body, scale, gravity, movement, collision, health or animation speed. Play Animation #

Plays an animation (a sequence of its model, by name or number) on a prop, an NPC or any model entity, at a speed (1 normal), from the start or not; Loop plays a one-time animation again every Length seconds (looping ones loop by themselves). Players, creatures and NPCs that are walking are animated by the game and will switch back.

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Entity Entity
The entity. Needs a wire.

Outputs

Goes on when it's done.

A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -). Default: idle.
Speed
A number from 0 to 10. Default: 1. From 0 up to 10.
From the start
On or off. Default: true.
Loop
On or off. Default: false.
Length (seconds, for Loop)
A number from 0.1 to 60. Default: 2. From 0.1 up to 60.

Limits: Looping animations at once (64 by default, hosts can raise it to 256; slow above 128).

Related: Set Grenade Fuse, Attach To, Detach

Makes an entity (a prop, a sprite, an NPC) follow a player or another entity, keeping an offset (X forward is along the world, not their facing) or, at an attachment point of their model (0 to 3), following that point. Let go when the parent dies
On or off. Default: true.

Limits: Attached things at once (64 by default, hosts can raise it to 256; slow above 128).

Spawn Entity #

Makes one of the game's entities with your keys (key=value pairs as in a map, | between them: model=sprites/glow01.spr|rendercolor=255 0 0|targetname=door_fx). Only classes that are safe to make while the game runs are listed: effects, sounds, markers, relays, pickups. Never players, worldspawn, game_* server controls or anything that runs commands. Files its keys name are loaded with the map; if one isn't on the server it's skipped, never a crash. Where: At entity (its spot, moved by Local offset along its own forward, right and up; it faces the way the entity does if Face the way At entity faces is on, turned by Rotation offset) or At position. Rotation (pitch, yaw, roll) sets the facing outright; Velocity starts it moving (a still thing then falls and slides); Scale sizes models and sprites.

Inputs

Runs this node.

Local offset (forward, right, up): a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Rotation (pitch, yaw, roll) Vector
Rotation (pitch, yaw, roll): a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Rotation offset Vector
Rotation offset: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Velocity Vector
Velocity: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Scale Number
Scale: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Entity Entity
The entity.

Settings

Class
Pick one: Sprite (env_sprite: model=sprites/...spr), Glow (env_glow: model=sprites/...spr), Animated sprite or model (cycler_sprite: model=...), Sparks (env_spark), Explosion (env_explosion: iMagnitude=100), Screen shake (env_shake), Screen fade (env_fade), Render changer (env_render), Blood spray (env_blood), Particle funnel (env_funnel), Soda can chute (env_beverage), Model shooter (env_shooter: shootmodel=models/...mdl)…. Default: env_sprite. Choices: Sprite (env_sprite: model=sprites/...spr), Glow (env_glow: model=sprites/...spr), Animated sprite or model (cycler_sprite: model=...), Sparks (env_spark), Explosion (env_explosion: iMagnitude=100), Screen shake (env_shake), Screen fade (env_fade), Render changer (env_render), Blood spray (env_blood), Particle funnel (env_funnel), Soda can chute (env_beverage), Model shooter (env_shooter: shootmodel=models/...mdl), Gib shooter (gibshooter), Beam (env_beam: texture=sprites/laserbeam.spr), Laser (env_laser: texture=sprites/laserbeam.spr), Sound (ambient_generic: message=ambience/x.wav), Target point (info_target), Path stop (path_corner), Relay (trigger_relay), Multi manager (multi_manager), Weapon on the ground (armoury_entity: item=0..29), Health kit (item_healthkit), Battery (item_battery).

Beam Between Entities #

A beam from one player or entity to another that follows both as they move, for some seconds. Effects are skipped (and the debugger says so) past 24 in one server frame, so many at once can't flood players' games.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
From Entity
From: an entity (a thing in the map). Needs a wire.
To Entity
To: an entity (a thing in the map). Needs a wire.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Color
Pick one: Red, Green, Blue, White, Gold. Default: red. Choices: Red, Green, Blue, White, Gold.
Seconds
A number from 0.1 to 25. Default: 2. From 0.1 up to 25.
Width
A number from 1 to 100. Default: 10. From 1 up to 100.
Wobble
A number from 0 to 100. Default: 0. From 0 up to 100.

Limits: Effect messages a frame (48 by default, hosts can raise it to 160; slow above 96).

Careful: Each beam is a temporary effect in every player's game, which has a shared limit; past 24 effects in one server frame the rest are skipped.

Related: Icon Over Player, Sprite At, Stop Sound

A number from 0 to 600. Default: 0. From 0 up to 600.
Height above
A number from 0 to 200. Default: 45. From 0 up to 200.
Seen by
Pick one: Everyone, Terrorists, Counter-Terrorists, Only Viewer. Default: all. Choices: Everyone, Terrorists, Counter-Terrorists, Only Viewer.

Advanced

Run Server Command #

Runs a server console command, like sv_gravity 400 or mp_restartgame 1. Commands that touch passwords, files, plugins or quit the server are refused, and only one command at a time (no ; ). Community modes that use it show "runs server commands".

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Command Text
Command: text. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Ran Yes/No
Ran: yes or no.

Settings

Command
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: sv_gravity 800. Up to 100.

Careful: Only gameplay settings and a short list of safe commands are allowed; anything else is refused and the node's OK is No. A community mode that has this shows "runs server commands".

Related: Set Setting, Run AMX Command, Call Plugin Function

Run AMX Command #

Runs an AMX Mod X admin command (amx_slay, amx_slap...). amx_rcon, amx_cvar on passwords, plugin loading, bans and admin edits are refused.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Command Text
Command: text. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Ran Yes/No
Ran: yes or no.

Settings

Command
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: amx_slap @T 0. Up to 100.

Careful: Only slap, slay, kick, say commands, map changes to real maps and allowed settings are let through. A community mode with this shows "runs AMX commands".

Related: Run Server Command, Call Plugin Function, Plugin Is Running

Call Plugin Function #

Calls a public function in another AMX Mod X plugin (by its file, like gm_components.amxx) with up to two numbers; Result is what it returns. Community modes that use it show "calls other plugins".

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Argument 1 Number
Argument 1: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Argument 2 Number
Argument 2: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Result Number
Result: a number.
Called Yes/No
Called: yes or no.

Settings

Plugin file
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -). Default: gm_components.amxx.
Function
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -). Default: plugin_cfg.
Arguments
Pick one: None, One, Two. Default: 0. Choices: None, One, Two.
Argument 1
A number from -100000 to 100000. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 0. From -100000 up to 100000.
Argument 2
A number from -100000 to 100000. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 0. From -100000 up to 100000.

Careful: Calls into another plugin. If that plugin or function isn't there, OK is No and nothing happens. A community mode with this shows "calls other plugins".

Related: Plugin Is Running, Fire Custom Event, On Custom Event

On or off. Default: true.

Say and Choose #

The NPC (or a narrator, with none wired) says a line and the player picks a reply; each reply runs its output (later, when they pick), and Picked says which (1-6; 0 for none). Timed out: no pick in time. Walked away: they went off, died, left or closed it. Every text can be wired (a variable, a table cell), so one node can run a whole conversation from a data table; Replies sets how many are offered (empty ones are left out). One conversation per player: a new one ends the old (as Walked away).

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Player Player
The player. Needs a wire.
NPC Entity
NPC: an entity (a thing in the map). Optional.
Speaker Text
Speaker: text. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Line Text
Line: text. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Reply 1 Text
Reply 1: text. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Reply 2 Text
Reply 2: text. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Reply 3 Text
Reply 3: text. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Reply 4 Text
Reply 4: text. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Reply 5 Text
Reply 5: text. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Reply 6 Text
Reply 6: text. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Replies Number
Replies: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Time to answer Number
Time to answer: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Reply 1 Flow
Runs on Reply 1.
Reply 2 Flow
Runs on Reply 2.
Reply 3 Flow
Runs on Reply 3.
Reply 4 Flow
Runs on Reply 4.
Reply 5 Flow
Runs on Reply 5.
Reply 6 Flow
Runs on Reply 6.
Timed out Flow
Runs on Timed out.
Walked away Flow
Runs on Walked away.
Picked Number
Picked: a number.

Settings

Speaker (empty: the NPC)
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Up to 31.
Line
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: Can you help me?. Up to 120.
Reply 1
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: Yes. Up to 40.
Reply 2
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: No. Up to 40.
Reply 3
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Up to 40.
Reply 4
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Up to 40.
Reply 5
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Up to 40.
Reply 6
Text: letters, digits, spaces and plain punctuation. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Up to 40.
Replies
A number from 1 to 6. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 6. From 1 up to 6.
Time to answer (0: no limit)
A number from 0 to 300. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 30. From 0 up to 300.

NPC Move To #

Sends an NPC walking (or running) to an entity or a point now, along the map's bot navigation (straight when the map has none), facing where it goes. Arrived runs when it gets there, Failed when it can't (no way, stuck, or too long). Then it goes back to what it did, or stays there.

Run Flow
Runs this node.
NPC Entity
NPC: an entity (a thing in the map). Needs a wire.
To entity Entity
To entity: an entity (a thing in the map). Optional.
Or point Vector
Or point: a position or direction (X, Y, Z).

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Arrived Flow
Runs on Arrived.
Failed Flow
Runs on Failed.

Settings

Speed
Pick one: Walk, Run. Default: walk. Choices: Walk, Run.
Stay there after
On or off. Default: false.

Limits: NPC moves at once (32 by default, hosts can raise it to 64; slow above 48).

Needs Loner-Strike's components plugin, which comes with the app.

Related: NPC Follow Path, NPC Stop, Hostage Follow

Or path_track (first name)

Loop
On or off. Default: false.
Stay at the end
On or off. Default: false.

Data tables

Get Row #

Finds the row whose key is this (the table's key column) and gives each of its cells. Found is No when no row has that key (the cells are then 0, No or empty).

Data tables (My mode > Data tables) are grids of values you type or paste from a spreadsheet: a gun list, prices, waves. Get Row finds the row whose key column has this value and gives every cell of it as a pin.

Inputs

Key Anything
The value to look for in the key column.

Outputs

Found Yes/No
Yes when a row has that key.
Row Number
The row's number (from 0), or -1 when none has the key.

Settings

Table
Picked from this mode's own pieces.

Related: Get Cell, For Each Row, Find Rows

Get Cell #

One cell: a column of a row (rows count from 0). Past the end: 0, No or empty.

Reads one cell. Rows count from 0, so the first row is 0; a row past the end gives 0, No or empty text.

Inputs

Settings

Picked from this mode's own pieces.

Picked from this mode's own pieces.

A number from 0 to 255. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 0. From 0 up to 255.

Related: Get Row, Row Count, For Each Row

For Each Row #

Runs Each once for every row of the table, in order, with that row's cells; then Then.

Runs Each for every row in order, with that row's cells as pins (and Row, its number). Then runs once after the last.

Runs this node.

Outputs

Runs once for every item.

Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Picked from this mode's own pieces.

Cost: A table has at most 256 rows, and reading one costs almost nothing: the plugin holds tables as fixed lists.

Related: Get Cell, Find Rows, Get Row

Projectiles

Spawn Projectile #

Fires a model or sprite (loaded with the map). Where from: At position, else At entity (a player's eyes) moved by Local offset along its own forward, right and up (16 forward when nothing is wired there), else the owner's eyes. Which way: Direction, else Rotation, else the way At entity (or the owner) faces when Face the way At entity faces is on, turned by Rotation offset; Velocity, when wired, sets the motion outright. Gravity 0 flies straight; 1 falls like a grenade. Bounces: off walls before it stops. Damage: to what it hits, or everything within Radius (less further out with Falloff). Kills count for the owner. On Hit runs later for this projectile only, with what it hit (each bounce too); On Expired when its time runs out. 64 fly at most; a new round clears them.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Owner Player
Owner: a player. Optional.
At entity Entity
At entity: an entity (a thing in the map). Optional.
At position Vector
At position: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Local offset (forward, right, up) Vector
Local offset (forward, right, up): a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Rotation (pitch, yaw, roll) Vector
Rotation (pitch, yaw, roll): a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Rotation offset Vector
Rotation offset: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Velocity Vector
Velocity: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Direction Vector
Direction: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
Speed Number
Speed: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Damage Number
Damage: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.
Scale Number
Scale: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Projectile Entity
Projectile: an entity (a thing in the map).
On Hit Flow
Runs on On Hit.
On Expired Flow
Runs on On Expired.
Hit Position Vector
Hit Position: a position or direction (X, Y, Z).
Hit Normal Vector
Hit Normal: a position or direction (X, Y, Z).
Hit Player Player
Hit Player: a player.
Hit Entity Entity
Hit Entity: an entity (a thing in the map).
Hit World Yes/No
Hit World: yes or no.
Hit Zone Number
Hit Zone: a number.
Owner Player
Owner: a player.

Settings

Model or sprite
A file in the game folder, like models/w_c4.mdl or ambience/thunder_clap.wav. Default: models/w_hegrenade.mdl.
Face the way At entity faces
On or off. Default: true.
Speed
A number from 1 to 5000. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 1000. From 1 up to 5000.
Scale
A number from 0.05 to 4. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 1. From 0.05 up to 4.
Gravity
A number from 0 to 4. Default: 0. From 0 up to 4.
Lifetime (seconds)
A number from 0.1 to 60. Default: 5. From 0.1 up to 60.
Bounces
A number from 0 to 10. Default: 0. From 0 up to 10.
Damage
A number from 0 to 5000. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 0. From 0 up to 5000.
Radius
A number from 0 to 2000. Default: 0. From 0 up to 2000.
Falloff
On or off. Default: true.
Explosion
On or off. Default: false.
Trail
On or off. Default: false.
Spin
On or off. Default: false.
Dangerous to bots
On or off. Default: false.

Limits: Projectiles at once (64 by default, hosts can raise it to 512; slow above 128); Effect messages a frame (48 by default, hosts can raise it to 160; slow above 96).

Needs Loner-Strike's components plugin, which comes with the app.

Related: Projectile Hit, Remove Projectile, Projectile Velocity

HUD & View

Hide HUD Parts #

Hides parts of a player's HUD (or everyone's): the crosshair, money, the round timer, the radar with health and armor (one switch in the game), the weapon and ammo panel, the flashlight icon. Nothing ticked shows it all again. It stays through the game redrawing the HUD; Back to normal on death/respawn ends it there.

Uses the game's own HUD switches (HideWeapon), so it costs nothing while it's on. The radar, health and armor are one switch in Counter-Strike. A spectator's own HUD isn't touched.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Player (empty: everyone) Player
Player (empty: everyone): a player. Optional.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Crosshair
On or off. Default: true.
Money
On or off. Default: false.
Round timer
On or off. Default: false.
Radar, health and armor
On or off. Default: false.
Weapon and ammo panel
On or off. Default: false.
Flashlight icon
On or off. Default: false.
Back to normal on death/respawn
On or off. Default: true.

Related: Set Field of View, Show HUD Text, Set Weapon View Model

Set Field of View #

How wide a player sees (or everyone): 90 is normal, less zooms in, more is a fish-eye. A sniper scope still zooms and comes back to this. Back to normal on death/respawn puts 90 back there.

Scopes still work: a sniper zooms in and goes back to this field of view. Very wide or very narrow views can make people feel sick; keep it between 60 and 120 for long play.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Player (empty: everyone) Player
Player (empty: everyone): a player. Optional.
Degrees Number
Degrees: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Degrees
A number from 10 to 170. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 90. From 10 up to 170.
Back to normal on death/respawn
On or off. Default: true.

Related: Hide HUD Parts, Set Weapon View Model, Flashlight

Set Weapon View Model #

The model a player sees in their own hands (models/v_...mdl) for the weapon in hand or a chosen weapon, kept whenever they take it out again; also, if set, the model others see them hold (models/p_...mdl). Back to normal model puts the game's own back. Your own models are loaded with the map and go with the mode when you publish it.

The model must be a file under models/ (the game's own, like models/v_knife.mdl, or your own). A file that isn't there is left out and the weapon keeps its own model. Only the look changes: damage, speed and sounds are the weapon's.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Player Player
The player. Needs a wire.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Settings

Weapon
Pick one: The one in hand, Knife, AK-47, M4A1, AWP, Scout, FAMAS, Galil, AUG, SG 552, MP5, P90…. Default: current. Choices: The one in hand, Knife, AK-47, M4A1, AWP, Scout, FAMAS, Galil, AUG, SG 552, MP5, P90, UMP 45, MAC-10, TMP, M3 shotgun, XM1014 shotgun, M249, Desert Eagle, USP, Glock, P228, Five-SeveN, Dual Elites, HE grenade, Flashbang, Smoke grenade.
View model
A file in the game folder, like models/w_c4.mdl or ambience/thunder_clap.wav. Default: models/v_knife.mdl.
Model others see (empty: unchanged)
A file in the game folder, like models/w_c4.mdl or ambience/thunder_clap.wav.
Back to normal model
On or off. Default: false.

Careful: Each model the mode uses is loaded with the map (the game has a limit of 512 models).

Related: Set Field of View, Flashlight, Hide HUD Parts

Flashlight #

Turns a player's flashlight on or off, lets them use it or not (F), and sets its battery (0 to 100; -1 leaves it).

Don't let them use it also turns it off; Let them use it undoes that. The battery is what the HUD shows; it drains and recharges as usual.

A number from -1 to 100. Default: -1. From -1 up to 100.

Limits: Effect messages a frame (48 by default, hosts can raise it to 160; slow above 96).

Scoreboard Team Color #

Shows a player as a Terrorist, Counter-Terrorist or spectator in everyone's game (name color, scoreboard section) without moving them: their real team, spawns and rules stay. Real team undoes it. Side effects: the radar and name tags follow what's shown, and the player's own buy menu and team HUD do too.

Only how the player is shown changes. The game still treats them as their real team for damage, spawns, buying and winning. Everyone's radar and name tags follow the shown team, and the player's own client does too (their buy menu shows that team's guns, which they can't buy).

Inputs

Limits: Effect messages a frame (48 by default, hosts can raise it to 160; slow above 96).

Careful: Players can be confused about who's on which team; say so in the mode's description.

Related: Scoreboard Tag, Force Spectate, Silent Footsteps

Makes a dead player or a spectator watch Target through their eyes, and keeps them on Target until Release (or Target leaves). A living player is refused.

The watcher sees through Target's eyes and can't switch away while it lasts. When Target dies they keep watching the body until Release or a new Target.

Plays a Half-Life soundtrack track for everyone or one player (the game's own music files, as a map's trigger_cdaudio does), looping if asked; or stops it. Players hear it only if their game has the soundtrack (Steam's Half-Life files do).

The tracks are the Half-Life soundtrack's, numbered as the game numbers them (2 is the first). It's the player's own music volume (MP3 volume in the options).

The radar only has room for a few dots: four hostage dots (slots 1 to 4) and one dropped-bomb dot. On a hostage map the real hostages use the same kind of dots. The game redraws the bomb dot when the real bomb is dropped or picked up.
