Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Done Yes/No
Done: yes or no.

Settings

Takes damage
On or off. Default: true.

Related: Set Entity Size, Entity Health, Set Entity Collision

Entity Health #

An entity's health (a prop, an NPC, a creature, a player).

Inputs

Entity Entity
The entity. Needs a wire.

Outputs

Health Number
Health: a number.

Related: Set Entity Health, Entity Takes Damage, Set Entity Size

Set Entity Health #

Sets a prop's or an NPC's health (at least 1; to destroy it use its own damage or removal). Not for players (Set Health is theirs).

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Entity Entity
The entity. Needs a wire.
Health Number
Health: a number. Leave it empty to use the setting.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Done Yes/No
Done: yes or no.

Settings

Health
A number from 1 to 1e+06. A wire into the pin of the same name is used instead. Default: 100. From 1 up to 1000000.

Related: Entity Health, Spawn Trigger Volume, Entity Takes Damage

Spawn Trigger Volume #

An invisible box or sphere that tells you who goes in and out (Begin Overlap, End Overlap). Where: a Position, or At entity plus a Local offset (X forward, Y right, Z up by its facing) - attached, it follows the entity and turns with it. Rotation (pitch, yaw, roll) turns a box; a sphere ignores it. Size is its own setting; Velocity and Scale don't apply (a volume doesn't move by itself). Who counts: players, creatures or anything. Checked ten times a second (one search round each volume). A name lets Player Touched and the overlap events pick it out. At most 32 at once.

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Position Vector
The position.
Rotation Vector
Rotation: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.
At entity Entity
At entity: an entity (a thing in the map). Optional.
Local offset Vector
Local offset: a position or direction (X, Y, Z). Optional.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.
Volume Entity
Volume: an entity (a thing in the map).

Settings

Shape
Pick one: Box, Sphere. Default: box. Choices: Box, Sphere.
Width (box)
A number from 8 to 8192. Default: 128. From 8 up to 8192.
Depth (box)
A number from 8 to 8192. Default: 128. From 8 up to 8192.
Height (box)
A number from 8 to 8192. Default: 96. From 8 up to 8192.
Radius (sphere)
A number from 8 to 4096. Default: 96. From 8 up to 4096.
Who counts
Pick one: Players, Creatures, Anything. Default: players. Choices: Players, Creatures, Anything.
Name
A name, as the map or the game knows it (letters, digits, _ . -).

Limits: Trigger volumes at once (32 by default, hosts can raise it to 256; slow above 64); Things inside one trigger volume (32 by default, hosts can raise it to 64; slow above 48).

Related: Set Entity Health, Remove Trigger Volume, Entity Health

Remove Trigger Volume #

Removes a trigger volume (whoever was inside gets End Overlap).

Inputs

Run Flow
Runs this node.
Volume Entity
Volume: an entity (a thing in the map). Needs a wire.

Outputs

Then Flow
Goes on when it's done.

Related: Spawn Trigger Volume, Is Overlapping, Set Entity Health

Is Overlapping #

Is this entity (a player, a creature...) inside the trigger volume right now (and one its filter counts)?

Inputs

Volume Entity
Volume: an entity (a thing in the map). Needs a wire.
Entity Entity
The entity. Needs a wire.

Outputs

Overlapping Yes/No
Overlapping: yes or no.

Related: Remove Trigger Volume, Spawn Trigger Volume, Set Entity Health

Built-in lists

Lists every mode has (What Kind Of, Component Kind Of give them; Switch on Enum fills its cases from them).

Entity Kind

Player, Bot, Creature, NPC, Interactable, Component, Prop, Map entity, None

Component Kind

Capture point, Extraction zone, Checkpoint, Shop, Barricade, Trap, Loot container, Supply drop, Supply crate, Perk machine, Upgrade bench, Mystery box, Meeting point, Moving platform, Timed door, Wave spawner, Safe zone, Shrinking zone, Damage zone, Weapon spawner, Respawn point, Player class, Pickup, NPC, Interactable, None

Starter library

Ready-made pieces to add from Game Mode Studio's My library.

Run Dialog

NPCs

A conversation from the Dialog table: line after line, each reply going to its Next row, until end. Fill the table's rows; wire Talked To NPC in.

In: In, NPC, Player, Start row. Out: Ended, Last row.

Ability with Cooldown

Starter: abilities

A template for any ability: Use runs when it's ready, Not ready says the seconds left; Bot Wants Ability for bots.

In: In, Player, Cooldown. Out: Use, Not ready, Seconds left.

Capture Zone

Starter: rounds and scoring

A capture point at an entity that a team takes by standing in it for 10 seconds; bots are sent to take it.

In: At. Out: Zone.

Countdown HUD

Starter: rounds and scoring

Counts down Seconds (0: 60) on everyone's HUD as minutes:seconds, then Done.

In: In, Seconds, Label. Out: Started, Done.

Dash

Starter: movement

A quick dash the way the player looks (Speed 0: 700), at most once every Cooldown seconds (0: 3).

In: In, Player, Speed, Cooldown. Out: Dashed, Not ready, Seconds left.

Double Jump

Starter: movement

A second jump in the air (Boost 0: 320 up), once until the player lands again.

In: In, Player, Boost. Out: Jumped, Not now.

Fireball

Starter: abilities

Throws a fireball where the player aims; it explodes where it hits (Damage 0: 60, Speed 0: 1100).

In: In, Player, Damage, Speed. Out: Thrown.

Health Pack

Starter: pickups

A health pack on a trigger volume: heals (0: 50, up to 100) and comes back after Respawn seconds (0: 20).

In: In, Volume, Who, Heal, Respawn after. Out: Taken, Not taken.

Hot Potato

Starter: spectacle

A bomb one player holds for Seconds (0: 20): a hit passes it on, and when time runs out it blows up its holder.

In: Player, Seconds. Out: .

Jump Pad

Starter: movement

Launches a player up (Speed, 0: 800) with a sound: call it from a pad's Begin Overlap.

In: Who, Speed. Out: .

Kill Cam

Starter: spectacle

Shows the victim the killer for Seconds (0: 3), from behind the killer, then back.

In: In, Victim, Killer, Seconds. Out: Then.

Kill Streak Announcer

Starter: rounds and scoring

Counts kills in a row per player and announces 3, 5, 7 and 10 to everyone; a death resets it.

In: Killer, Victim. Out: Streak.

Leaderboard Display

Starter: rounds and scoring

Shows a player the top players of a saved score (Key, e.g. kills) in an info window.

In: Player. Out: .

Lives Counter

Starter: player state

Each player's lives: Lose a Life on a death (with a HUD line), Out when none are left; Reset Lives at the start (0: 3).

In: Player. Out: Lives left, Out.

Proximity Mine

Starter: abilities

Places a mine at the player's feet that blows up when someone steps near (Radius 0: 96, Damage 0: 120).

In: Player, Radius, Damage. Out: Mine.

Quest Step Tracker

Starter: story

Each player's quest step (a word you choose, e.g. find_fuse) with a HUD line; pairs with Run Dialog's Last row.

In: Player, Step, Objective. Out: .

Spawn Protection

Starter: player state

God mode and a glow for Seconds (0: 3) after a player spawns; it ends early if they shoot.

In: In, Player, Seconds. Out: Then, Over.

Burn, Slow and Freeze

Starter: player state

Status effects with timers: Burn (Seconds 0: 4, Damage a second 0: 5), Slow (0: 4 s at 0: 150 speed) and Freeze (0: 2 s).

In: In, Player, By, Seconds, Damage a second. Out: Then.

Teleporter Pair

Starter: movement

Sends a player to a destination entity, at most once every Cooldown seconds (0: 2).

In: Who, To, Cooldown. Out: Went.

Victory Camera

Starter: spectacle

Everyone looks at the winner for Seconds (0: 5) at the end of a round.

In: In, Winner, Seconds. Out: Then.

Weapon Pickup

Starter: pickups

A weapon lying on a trigger volume: gives it (Weapon, e.g. awp) and comes back after Respawn seconds (0: 20).

In: In, Volume, Who, Weapon, Respawn after. Out: Taken, Not taken.
